🧭 Perception (3D / Tactile)Perception (3D / Tactile)

· 发展脉络· Development

该方向聚焦于具身智能中的感知核心，旨在解决机器人在复杂动态环境中对三维空间结构、物理属性及触觉交互的精准理解问题。发展脉络从早期的单一视觉或触觉模态感知，逐步演进为多模态融合（视觉-触觉-语言），并进一步向生成式世界模型与主动感知（Active Perception）转变。近期研究重点在于利用大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言动作模型（VLA）构建具身智能的“大脑”，通过引入触觉反馈、物理一致性约束及隐式空间表示，显著提升了机器人在接触式操作、长程任务规划及Sim2Real迁移中的鲁棒性。This direction focuses on the perception core in embodied intelligence, aiming to solve the problem of precise understanding of 3D spatial structures, physical attributes, and tactile interactions for robots in complex dynamic environments. The development trajectory has evolved from early single-modality perception (vision or touch) to multimodal fusion (vision-touch-language), and further towards generative world models and active perception. Recent research focuses on building the "brain" of embodied intelligence using large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action (VLA) models. By introducing tactile feedback, physical consistency constraints, and implicit spatial representations, robustness in contact-based manipulation, long-horizon task planning, and Sim2Real transfer has been significantly improved.

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

3D 视觉语言与空间推理增强3D Vision-Language and Spatial Reasoning Enhancement

该方向旨在解决 VLM/VLA 模型缺乏深度感知与空间推理能力的痛点，核心思路是从显式几何对齐到隐式特征蒸馏的演进。早期工作如 AffordanceLLM 和 RoboPoint 尝试通过外挂深度图或合成数据赋予模型基础空间指令能力，但多依赖外部传感器或 2D 投影。随后，SpatialBot 和 RoboSpatial 通过大规模 3D 数据预训练强化空间理解，但仍受限于深度估计的准确性。近期研究如 SpatialVLA、Spatial Forcing 和 OpenSPM 转向隐式空间表征对齐与轻量级几何先验，试图在不依赖重型 3D 传感器的情况下，通过知识蒸馏或结构化记忆让模型内化 3D 几何与动态信息，实现从“平面视觉”向“立体空间智能”的跨越。This direction aims to address the pain point of VLM/VLA models lacking depth perception and spatial reasoning capabilities, with the core idea evolving from explicit geometric alignment to implicit feature distillation. Early works such as AffordanceLLM and RoboPoint attempted to endow models with basic spatial instruction capabilities by attaching depth maps or synthetic data, but they mostly relied on external sensors or 2D projections. Subsequently, SpatialBot and RoboSpatial strengthened spatial understanding through large-scale 3D data pre-training, but were still limited by the accuracy of depth estimation. Recent research such as SpatialVLA, Spatial Forcing, and OpenSPM has shifted towards implicit spatial representation alignment and lightweight geometric priors, attempting to let models internalize 3D geometry and dynamic information through knowledge distillation or structured memory without relying on heavy 3D sensors, achieving a leap from "planar vision" to "stereoscopic spatial intelligence".

2024-06
RoboPoint: A Vision-Language Model for Spatial Affordance Prediction for Robotics
动机: Motivation: 解决机器人对模糊空间指令理解困难及依赖昂贵演示数据的问题。Addressing the difficulties in understanding ambiguous spatial instructions by robots and the reliance on expensive demonstration data.
方法: Method: 构建自动化合成数据流水线，训练 VLM 直接预测空间坐标。Building an automated synthetic data pipeline to train VLMs to directly predict spatial coordinates.
推进: Advance: 在空间定位精度上超越 GPT-4o 和 PIVOT，展现了处理未见空间关系的潜力。Surpassed GPT-4o and PIVOT in spatial localization accuracy, demonstrating potential in handling unseen spatial relationships.
2024-06
SpatialBot: Precise Spatial Understanding with Vision Language Models
动机: Motivation: 解决纯 RGB VLM 缺乏深度感知、无法判断物体间遮挡与距离的“平面眼”顽疾。Addressing the "planar eye" malady of pure RGB VLMs lacking depth perception and being unable to judge occlusion and distance between objects.
方法: Method: 强制将深度图作为额外模态输入，结合 ZoeDepth 进行空间理解训练。Forcing depth maps to be used as an additional modality input, combined with ZoeDepth for spatial understanding training.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了模型对空间关系（如谁摸到了谁）的判别能力，弥补了 VLM 的立体视觉短板。Significantly improved the model's discriminative ability for spatial relationships (e.g., who touched whom), making up for the stereoscopic vision shortcomings of VLMs.
2024-11
3D-Mem: 3D Scene Memory for Embodied Exploration and Reasoning
动机: Motivation: 解决具身智能在长程任务中缺乏持久场景记忆与空间关系理解的问题。Addressing the lack of persistent scene memory and spatial relationship understanding in Embodied AI during long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 利用多视角快照构建 3D 场景记忆，替代传统点云或文本描述。Using multi-view snapshots to build 3D scene memory, replacing traditional point clouds or text descriptions.
推进: Advance: 在 A-EQA 和 GOAT-Bench 上表现优异，证明了“相册式”记忆范式对空间推理的增强作用。Performed excellently on A-EQA and GOAT-Bench, proving the enhancing effect of the "album-style" memory paradigm on spatial reasoning.
2024-12
Thinking in Space: How Multimodal Large Language Models See, Remember, and Recall Spaces
动机: Motivation: 揭示 MLLM 在空间推理上的严重缺陷，特别是距离与方位判断的“平面近视”问题。Revealing serious defects in MLLMs in spatial reasoning, especially the "planar myopia" problem in distance and orientation judgment.
方法: Method: 构建 VSI-Bench 基准，系统评估 MLLM 的空间认知能力并测试 CoT 效果。Constructing the VSI-Bench benchmark to systematically evaluate the spatial cognitive capabilities of MLLMs and test the CoT effect.
推进: Advance: 发现 CoT 在空间任务中反而降低性能，提出显式生成认知地图是提升空间智商的关键。Found that CoT actually reduces performance in spatial tasks, proposing that explicitly generating cognitive maps is key to improving spatial intelligence.
2024-12
FlowPolicy: Enabling Fast and Robust 3D Flow-based Policy via Consistency Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有3D扩散策略推理速度慢、难以满足实时控制需求的问题，旨在通过优化采样机制提升3D流策略的效率。Aiming to address the slow inference speed of existing 3D diffusion strategies and their difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements, this work seeks to improve the efficiency of 3D flow strategies by optimizing the sampling mechanism.
方法: Method: 提出一致性流匹配（Consistency Flow Matching）替代传统扩散采样器，直接基于3D点云条件生成动作流。Proposes consistency flow matching to replace traditional diffusion samplers, directly generating action flows based on 3D point cloud conditions.
推进: Advance: 将推理时间从145ms大幅降低至20ms，在保持与DP3相当甚至更高成功率的同时，显著提升了3D视觉语言策略的实时响应能力。Reduces inference time from 145ms to 20ms, significantly enhancing the real-time response capability of 3D vision-language strategies while maintaining success rates comparable to or higher than DP3.
2025-01
SpatialVLA: Exploring Spatial Representations for Visual-Language-Action Model
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型缺乏 3D 深度感知导致的空间推理能力不足问题。Addressing the insufficient spatial reasoning capability of VLA models caused by the lack of 3D depth perception.
方法: Method: 引入 Ego3D 编码与自适应动作网格，将深度信息嵌入 VLA 推理过程。Introducing Ego3D encoding and adaptive action grids to embed depth information into the VLA reasoning process.
推进: Advance: 在 WidowX 和 Franka 上刷新 SOTA，证明了显式深度感知能显著提升 VLA 的推理速度与精度。Refreshed SOTA on WidowX and Franka, proving that explicit depth perception can significantly improve the reasoning speed and accuracy of VLA.
2025-01
GPT4Scene: Understand 3D Scenes from Videos with Vision-Language Models
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLM 直接处理点云困难，需绕过复杂对齐问题实现 3D 场景理解。Addressing the difficulty of VLMs directly processing point clouds and needing to bypass complex alignment problems to achieve 3D scene understanding.
方法: Method: 利用视频重建生成 BEV 图与标记，作为 VLM 的“带坐标地图”输入。Using video reconstruction to generate BEV maps and annotations as "coordinate maps" input for VLMs.
推进: Advance: 实现了零样本 3D 场景理解，证明了纯视觉方案绕过点云对齐的可行性与实用性。Achieved zero-shot 3D scene understanding, proving the feasibility and practicality of pure vision solutions bypassing point cloud alignment.
2025-08
MolmoAct: Action Reasoning Models that can Reason in Space
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型仅做感知到动作映射，缺乏真正的空间推理与可解释性。Addressing the lack of true spatial reasoning and interpretability in VLA models that only perform perception-to-action mapping.
方法: Method: 设计三阶段流水线（深度计算-轨迹绘制-动作生成），引入 VQVAE 量化深度。Designing a three-stage pipeline (depth calculation - trajectory drawing - action generation) and introducing VQVAE to quantize depth.
推进: Advance: 在 SimplerEnv 上超越 π0 和 GR00T，展示了显式空间推理链条对提升动作规划的有效性。Surpassed π0 and GR00T on SimplerEnv, demonstrating the effectiveness of explicit spatial reasoning chains in improving action planning.
2025-10
Spatial Forcing: Implicit Spatial Representation Alignment for Vision-language-action Model
动机: Motivation: 解决 2D VLA 模型缺乏 3D 感知且直接引入深度传感器成本高的问题。Addressing the lack of 3D perception in 2D VLA models and the high cost of directly introducing depth sensors.
方法: Method: 提出 Spatial Forcing，利用 VGGT 的 3D 几何表示监督 VLA 中间层特征对齐。Proposing Spatial Forcing, using VGGT's 3D geometric representation to supervise the alignment of VLA intermediate layer features.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需 3D 输入推理的隐式空间能力，训练效率提升 3.8 倍且在 LIBERO 上刷榜。Achieved implicit spatial capabilities without 3D input inference, improving training efficiency by 3.8 times and topping the leaderboard on LIBERO.
2026-06
MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator
动机: Motivation: 解决 RGB-only 导航缺乏 3D 结构感知导致长距离漂移与规划失败的问题。Addressing the problem of long-distance drift and planning failure in RGB-only navigation due to the lack of 3D structural perception.
方法: Method: 利用 VGGT 将 2D 图像映射为 3D 框，构建多层价值地图进行导航规划。Using VGGT to map 2D images to 3D bounding boxes and constructing multi-layer value maps for navigation planning.
推进: Advance: 在 HM3D 上显著超越 PanoNav，证明了“物理感知 + 语义规划”缝合方案的有效性。Significantly surpassed PanoNav on HM3D, proving the effectiveness of the "physical perception + semantic planning" stitching solution.
2026-06
Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds
动机: Motivation: 解决机器人相机控制只顾平滑轨迹而缺乏基于语义意图的视觉注意力问题。Addressing the problem that robot camera control only focuses on smooth trajectories and lacks visual attention based on semantic intentions.
方法: Method: 构建叙事驱动的视觉注意力框架，将观察意图与运动执行解耦。Construct a narrative-driven visual attention framework that decouples observation intent from motion execution.
推进: Advance: 在 3D 故事世界中实现了更精准的视觉约束，避免了“为了动而动”的盲目性。Achieved more precise visual constraints in 3D story worlds, avoiding the blindness of 'moving for the sake of moving'.
2026-06
Ordinal Neural Collapse as a Representation Prior for Visual Navigation
动机: Motivation: 解决端到端视觉导航编码器特征模糊、在复杂路口容易迷路的问题。Address the issue of blurred encoder features in end-to-end visual navigation and the tendency to get lost at complex intersections.
方法: Method: 引入序数神经坍缩（ONC）作为表示先验，强制特征空间在几何上有序排列。Introduce Ordinal Neural Collapse (ONC) as a representation prior to enforce geometrically ordered arrangement in the feature space.
推进: Advance: 显著降低了 Heading Jerk，在复杂路口导航中提升了稳定性与鲁棒性。Significantly reduced Heading Jerk, improving stability and robustness in navigation at complex intersections.
2026-06
SSI-Policy: Learning Structured Scene Interfaces for Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型依赖深度相机且数据稀缺，需解耦感知与控制的问题。Address the problem of VLA models relying on depth cameras and data scarcity, necessitating the decoupling of perception and control.
方法: Method: 提出 SSI 中间层，将深度、布局与轨迹打包输入扩散策略，无需深度相机。Propose the SSI intermediate layer to bundle depth, layout, and trajectory inputs into diffusion policies without requiring depth cameras.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 上超越 ATM-DP，证明了结构化场景接口在少样本学习中的优势。Surpassed ATM-DP on LIBERO, demonstrating the advantages of structured scene interfaces in few-shot learning.
2026-06
Occ-VLM: Occupancy Grounded Vision Language Model for Indoor Scene Understanding
动机: Motivation: 解决单目 RGB 场景理解缺乏 3D 几何先验，且传统 3D 编码器成本过高的问题。Address the lack of 3D geometric priors in monocular RGB scene understanding and the excessive cost of traditional 3D encoders.
方法: Method: 利用 Occupancy 作为几何先验，将 2D 特征提升至 3D 空间进行统一推理。Utilize Occupancy as a geometric prior to lift 2D features into 3D space for unified reasoning.
推进: Advance: 在不依赖点云的情况下实现了 SOTA 的 3D 几何感知，降低了传感器成本。Achieved SOTA 3D geometric perception without relying on point clouds, reducing sensor costs.
2026-06
OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection
动机: Motivation: 解决多视角 3D 理解需复杂架构训练，且老模型难以直接利用 3D 信息的问题。Address the issue that multi-view 3D understanding requires complex architecture training and that older models cannot directly utilize 3D information.
方法: Method: 将多视角特征投影至全景画布，伪装成单张图输入现有 VLM。Project multi-view features onto a panoramic canvas, disguising them as a single image for input into existing VLMs.
推进: Advance: 实现了高效的“降维打击”，证明了输入格式优化可让老模型具备 3D 理解能力。Achieved an efficient 'dimensionality reduction strike', proving that input format optimization can equip older models with 3D understanding capabilities.
2026-06
Sequential Planning via Anchored Robotic Keypoints
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型死记硬背轨迹，在空间扰动下缺乏自适应规划能力的问题。Address the problem of VLA models memorizing trajectories by rote and lacking adaptive planning capabilities under spatial perturbations.
方法: Method: 提出 SPARK，利用神经符号架构将计划固定为行为树，动态重定位关键点。Propose SPARK, utilizing a neuro-symbolic architecture to fix plans as behavior trees and dynamically relocalize key points.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上显著降低算力消耗，证明了显式行为树比端到端黑盒更适应空间扰动。Significantly reduced computational consumption on LIBERO-PRO, proving that explicit behavior trees are more adaptable to spatial perturbations than end-to-end black boxes.
2026-07
Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs
动机: Motivation: 解决第一人称视频理解中隐式特征难以解释、长程推理能力弱的问题。Address the problem of implicit features being hard to interpret and weak long-range reasoning capabilities in first-person video understanding.
方法: Method: 构建场景图 SG-Ego 数据集，将视频理解转化为显式的图编辑推理。Construct the SG-Ego scene graph dataset, transforming video understanding into explicit graph editing reasoning.
推进: Advance: 在 EgoSchema 上超越部分 MLLM，证明了结构化场景图在长程推理中的可解释性优势。Surpassed some MLLMs on EgoSchema, proving the interpretability advantages of structured scene graphs in long-range reasoning.
2026-07
Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models What to See and Where to Look
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型注意力分散、缺乏明确空间 grounding 导致导航失败的问题。Address the problem of VLA models suffering from scattered attention and lack of explicit spatial grounding, leading to navigation failures.
方法: Method: 提出双模型架构，利用 TGP-Prompter 指引视觉注意力，TGP-Planner 负责规划。Propose a dual-model architecture, utilizing TGP-Prompter to guide visual attention and TGP-Planner to handle planning.
推进: Advance: 在 NAVSIM 上 PDMS 达 90.4%，证明了“看”与“开”解耦能显著提升空间感知效率。Achieved a PDMS of 90.4% on NAVSIM, proving that decoupling 'seeing' and 'driving' can significantly improve spatial perception efficiency.
2026-07
DeWorldSG: Depth-Aware 3D Semantic Scene Graph Generation via World-Model Priors
动机: Motivation: 解决传统 3D 场景图物体位置飘忽、关系漏判，且依赖静态检测的问题。Address the issues of traditional 3D scene graphs having fluctuating object positions, missed relationships, and reliance on static detection.
方法: Method: 利用 V-JEPA 2 世界模型先验，将物体建模为概率 3D 高斯分布生成场景图。Utilize V-JEPA 2 world model priors to model objects as probabilistic 3D Gaussian distributions for generating scene graphs.
推进: Advance: 关系召回率暴涨 77%，证明了世界模型先验对提升 3D 场景图稳健性的作用。Relationship recall surged by 77%, proving the role of world model priors in enhancing the robustness of 3D scene graphs.
2026-07
Lift3D-VLA: Lifting VLA Models to 3D Geometry and Dynamics-Aware Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型缺乏 3D 几何与动态感知，且大规模 3D 数据稀缺的问题。Address the problem of VLA models lacking 3D geometric and dynamic perception, coupled with the scarcity of large-scale 3D data.
方法: Method: 将点云投影至虚拟平面复用 2D 编码，利用 GC-MAE 进行重建与预测。Project point clouds onto virtual planes to reuse 2D encoding, utilizing GC-MAE for reconstruction and prediction.
推进: Advance: 实现了轻量级 3D 提升策略，证明了“借尸还魂”的 2D 方案在缺乏数据时的有效性。Achieved a lightweight 3D enhancement strategy, proving the effectiveness of the 'borrowing a corpse to return to life' 2D approach in data-scarce scenarios.
2026-07
Do Egocentric Video-Language Models Capture Both Hand- and Object-Centric Cues?
动机: Motivation: 现有自视（Egocentric）VLM 模型常依赖背景线索或静态物体位置进行“作弊”式推理，缺乏对遮挡场景下手物交互动态的真实感知能力。Existing egocentric VLM models often rely on background cues or static object positions for 'cheating' reasoning, lacking the true perception capability of hand-object interaction dynamics in occluded scenarios.
方法: Method: 提出 CI-HOI 评估框架，通过移除关键的手部或物体视觉信息（掩码），强制模型在缺乏显式几何线索下验证其推理鲁棒性。Proposes the CI-HOI evaluation framework, which removes key visual information of hands or objects (via masking) to force models to verify their reasoning robustness in the absence of explicit geometric cues.
推进: Advance: 揭示了模型在视觉线索缺失时的失效机制，证明了现有方法未真正内化 3D 空间交互，并验证了所提取特征在人类视频与机器人视角间的泛化潜力。Reveals the failure mechanisms of models when visual cues are missing, proving that existing methods have not truly internalized 3D spatial interactions, and validates the generalization potential of the extracted features between human videos and robot viewpoints.
2026-07
StoryTeller: Training-Free Narrative Grounding for Long-Form Audio Description
动机: Motivation: 针对长视频理解中缺乏连贯空间叙事与深度上下文记忆的问题，试图在无需额外训练的情况下增强模型对长序列中物体位置与交互的感知。Aiming to enhance models' perception of object positions and interactions in long sequences without additional training, addressing the lack of coherent spatial narrative and deep contextual memory in long video understanding.
方法: Method: 引入显著性记忆机制与身份图，利用 VLM 进行事实验证来构建连贯的长程音频描述，而非依赖传统的字幕或角色库。Introducing a saliency memory mechanism and identity maps, utilizing VLMs for fact verification to construct coherent long-range audio descriptions, rather than relying on traditional subtitles or character libraries.
推进: Advance: 通过显式的记忆结构将长视频中的空间实体关联起来，弥补了传统片段拼接式理解在空间连续性上的缺失，但推理成本较高。Linking spatial entities in long videos through explicit memory structures, addressing the lack of spatial continuity in traditional clip-based understanding, but at a high inference cost.
2026-07
See like a Robot: Robot-Centric Pointmaps for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型中相机坐标系与机器人基座坐标系不一致导致的“空间精神分裂”问题，特别是在视角多变场景下无法准确执行空间指令。Addressing the 'spatial schizophrenia' problem caused by the inconsistency between the camera coordinate system and the robot base coordinate system in VLA models, particularly the inability to accurately execute spatial instructions in scenarios with varying viewpoints.
方法: Method: 提出机器人中心点图（Robot-Centric Pointmap），将深度图转换为机器人坐标系下的图像表示，直接以像素存储 XYZ 坐标，保留 2D 网格结构。Proposing a Robot-Centric Pointmap, converting depth maps into image representations under the robot coordinate system, directly storing XYZ coordinates in pixels while preserving the 2D grid structure.
推进: Advance: 在不破坏 2D 预训练模型架构的前提下，实现了从视觉感知到机器人动作空间的无缝几何对齐，消除了视角歧义。Achieving seamless geometric alignment from visual perception to robot action space without disrupting the 2D pre-trained model architecture, eliminating viewpoint ambiguity.

3D-DefectBench: A Controlled Factorial Study of Vision-Language Model Evaluation Pipelines for Fine-Grained 3D Generation Defects
动机: Motivation: 揭示现有 VLM 在 3D 缺陷评估中过度依赖昂贵深度传感器且评估结果不可靠的痛点，试图通过受控因子实验厘清评估成本与精度的关系。Revealing the pain point that existing VLMs overly rely on expensive depth sensors for 3D defect assessment with unreliable results, attempting to clarify the relationship between assessment cost and accuracy through controlled factor experiments.
方法: Method: 构建 3D-DefectBench 进行多配置因子研究，对比不同渲染方案与传感器模态（RGB vs 深度图）对 VLM 评估性能的影响。Constructing 3D-DefectBench for multi-configurational factor studies, comparing the impact of different rendering schemes and sensor modalities (RGB vs. depth maps) on VLM assessment performance.
推进: Advance: 实证发现低成本的多视角 RGB 方案在多数任务中优于深度图方案，推翻了“深度图必优”的假设，为轻量化空间感知评估提供了新基准。Empirically finding that low-cost multi-view RGB schemes outperform depth map schemes in most tasks, overturning the assumption that 'depth maps are always superior' and providing a new benchmark for lightweight spatial perception assessment.

Compositional Context Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Model for Complex Assembly Action Understanding from Videos
动机: Motivation: 针对复杂组装任务中 VLM 输出不确定且难以捕捉细微手物交互空间特征的问题，试图通过结构化约束提升空间推理的准确性。Addressing the issue of uncertain VLM outputs and difficulty in capturing subtle hand-object interaction spatial features in complex assembly tasks, attempting to improve spatial reasoning accuracy through structured constraints.
方法: Method: 采用组合上下文微调（CCFT）策略，将动作拆解为动词、物体、工具等原子元素，并通过分层模板问答强制模型输出确定性指令。Adopting a Compositional Context Fine-Tuning (CCFT) strategy, decomposing actions into atomic elements such as verbs, objects, and tools, and forcing the model to output deterministic instructions through hierarchical template question-answering.
推进: Advance: 证明了通过冻结视觉编码器并聚焦于高层语义组合，可在特定工业场景下显著提升空间动作的识别鲁棒性，但泛化能力受限于预定义模板。Demonstrating that freezing the visual encoder and focusing on high-level semantic composition can significantly improve the robustness of spatial action recognition in specific industrial scenarios, but generalization is limited by predefined templates.

Closing the Loop in Humanoid VLA: Persistent 3D Object Tokens for Verifiable Loco-Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统VLA模型缺乏长程记忆导致物体状态在长任务中丢失的问题，旨在通过显式固化物体状态来增强空间推理的连续性。Aiming to address the issue where traditional VLA models lack long-term memory, causing object states to be lost during long-horizon tasks, this work seeks to enhance the continuity of spatial reasoning by explicitly solidifying object states.
方法: Method: 提出Persistent 3D Object Tokens (POT) 机制，利用SAM3和深度相机将物体状态编码为持久化Token，实现机器人对物体位置与状态的显式记忆。Proposes the Persistent 3D Object Tokens (POT) mechanism, which utilizes SAM3 and depth cameras to encode object states into persistent tokens, enabling robots to explicitly memorize object positions and states.
推进: Advance: 在Unitree G1上显著提升了长程任务成功率，证明了通过外挂内存而非修改主干网络即可有效内化3D几何状态，实现了从瞬时感知到持久空间记忆的跨越。Significantly improved long-horizon task success rates on the Unitree G1, demonstrating that 3D geometric states can be effectively internalized via external memory rather than modifying the backbone network, achieving a leap from instantaneous perception to persistent spatial memory.

方法: Method: 设计基于可变形注意力机制的RGB-D融合Transformer，动态对齐纹理与几何特征点，实现深度与视觉信息的自适应融合。Designs an RGB-D fusion Transformer based on a deformable attention mechanism to dynamically align texture and geometric feature points, achieving adaptive fusion of depth and visual information.
推进: Advance: 在严重遮挡下实现了比传统早期/晚期融合更鲁棒的特征对齐，验证了动态几何对齐策略在提升空间感知精度上的有效性。Achieves more robust feature alignment under severe occlusion compared to traditional early/late fusion, validating the effectiveness of dynamic geometric alignment strategies in enhancing spatial perception accuracy.

GeoWorldAD: Geometry World Action Model for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 克服端到端自动驾驶规划中缺乏显式3D几何约束，导致规划与真实物理世界几何关系不匹配的问题。Overcomes the issue in end-to-end autonomous driving planning where the lack of explicit 3D geometric constraints leads to a mismatch between planning and the geometric relationships of the real physical world.
方法: Method: 构建GeoWorldAD几何世界模型，引入ego-aligned坐标系修正与未来几何预测，将显式3D几何先验直接融入端到端规划流。Constructs the GeoWorldAD geometric world model, introducing ego-aligned coordinate system correction and future geometric prediction to directly embed explicit 3D geometric priors into the end-to-end planning stream.
推进: Advance: 解决了流式重建与车辆运动不匹配的难题，证明了将显式几何约束作为“眼镜”嵌入VLA架构能显著提升规划的空间一致性与安全性。Solves the difficulty of mismatch between streaming reconstruction and vehicle motion, proving that embedding explicit geometric constraints as 'glasses' into the VLA architecture significantly enhances the spatial consistency and safety of planning.

PerceptDrive: Perception Prior World-Action Modeling with Adaptive Expert Routing for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 解决端到端自动驾驶中单一接口导致几何、语义与动态先验信息衰减，难以在开放世界泛化的问题。Addresses the problem in end-to-end autonomous driving where a single interface leads to the attenuation of geometric, semantic, and dynamic prior information, making generalization in open worlds difficult.
方法: Method: 提出PerceptDrive架构，利用专家路由机制将多模态先验分离存储，并通过场景自适应软门控进行融合，利用特权指标蒸馏优化路由。Proposes the PerceptDrive architecture, which utilizes an expert routing mechanism to separate and store multimodal priors, fuses them via scene-adaptive soft gating, and optimizes routing using privileged metric distillation.
推进: Advance: 实现了仅用单摄像头达到SOTA性能，证明了通过结构化路由显式管理几何与动态先验，比单一隐式表征更能有效利用空间信息。Achieves SOTA performance using only a single camera, proving that explicitly managing geometric and dynamic priors through structured routing is more effective in utilizing spatial information than single implicit representations.

ReferTrack: Referring Then Tracking for Embodied Visual Tracking
动机: Motivation: 针对具身视觉跟踪中识别与规划混同导致的监督困难与空间对齐差，旨在通过显式索引机制增强空间推理的可解释性。Aiming to address the difficulties in supervision and poor spatial alignment caused by the conflation of recognition and planning in embodied visual tracking, this work seeks to enhance the interpretability of spatial reasoning through an explicit indexing mechanism.
方法: Method: 提出ReferThenTracking范式，将目标识别转化为从检测框中“选索引”的离散Token操作，利用显式Grounding实现从检测到跟踪的规划。Proposes the ReferThenTracking paradigm, transforming target recognition into a discrete token operation of 'selecting indices' from detection boxes, utilizing explicit grounding to plan from detection to tracking.
推进: Advance: 通过简单的显式索引机制弥补了单摄像头视野不足，证明了将推理拉回图像空间并依赖离散Token进行空间定位，比抽象CoT更有效地利用了视觉几何信息。Compensates for the insufficient field of view of a single camera through a simple explicit indexing mechanism, proving that pulling reasoning back to image space and relying on discrete tokens for spatial localization utilizes visual geometric information more effectively than abstract Chain-of-Thought.

推进: Advance: 揭示了地图作为“资源分配器”而非“导航捷径”的理论本质，证明了在感知波动率驱动下，基于记忆的重感知能显著提升有限算力下的空间推理效率。Revealing the theoretical essence of maps as "resource allocators" rather than "navigation shortcuts," proving that re-perception based on memory can significantly enhance spatial reasoning efficiency under limited computing power driven by perception volatility.

方法: Method: 提出 ByDeWay-V2 框架，将 YOLO 检测框与深度图作为显式几何约束直接拼接输入，通过提示工程而非模型微调来引导推理。Proposes the ByDeWay-V2 framework, which directly concatenates YOLO detection boxes and depth maps as explicit geometric constraints for input, guiding reasoning through prompt engineering rather than model fine-tuning.
推进: Advance: 证明了在资源受限场景下，显式几何约束比单纯堆叠参数更能有效抑制幻觉，但代价是推理流水线复杂度增加及对外部检测器的强依赖。Demonstrates that in resource-constrained scenarios, explicit geometric constraints are more effective than simply stacking parameters in suppressing hallucinations, but at the cost of increased inference pipeline complexity and strong dependence on external detectors.

动机: Motivation: 解决VLM直接学习空间推理困难且易产生幻觉的问题，探索通过工具辅助再内化知识来提升空间推理精度的路径。Address the difficulty and hallucination issues of VLMs directly learning spatial reasoning, exploring a path to improve spatial reasoning accuracy by internalizing knowledge through tool assistance.

动机: Motivation: 解决全景视觉（ERP投影）导致的几何失真及方向漂移问题，增强VLM在360度空间中的定向与导航能力。Address the geometric distortion and directional drift caused by panoramic vision (ERP projection), enhancing the orientation and navigation capabilities of VLMs in 360-degree spaces.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型在 2D 语义理解与 3D 动作执行之间存在几何错位，导致空间推理幻觉的问题。Addressing the geometric misalignment between 2D semantic understanding and 3D action execution in existing VLA models, which leads to spatial reasoning hallucinations.
方法: Method: 提出 GeoCoTDrive 框架，采用“先 2D 思考后 3D 驱动”的显式几何思维链，利用冻结的几何模型提取关键区域特征并注入上下文。Proposing the GeoCoTDrive framework, which adopts an explicit geometric chain-of-thought of 'think 2D first, then drive 3D', utilizing frozen geometric models to extract key region features and inject them into the context.
推进: Advance: 通过显式几何约束有效降低了自动驾驶场景下的碰撞率，证明了在无需全局深度感知的情况下，局部几何推理能显著提升 VLA 的空间准确性。Effectively reducing collision rates in autonomous driving scenarios through explicit geometric constraints, demonstrating that local geometric reasoning can significantly enhance the spatial accuracy of VLA models without requiring global depth perception.

Beyond Anonymous Captions: Grounding Character Identity in Video Captioning and Question Answering
动机: Motivation: 解决视频理解模型缺乏对实体身份与空间位置关联的感知能力，导致在复杂场景下空间推理模糊的问题。Addressing the lack of perception capability regarding entity identity and spatial location association in video understanding models, leading to ambiguous spatial reasoning in complex scenes.

Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching
动机: Motivation: 填补 UMI 类具身教学范式中缺乏高精度末端执行器定位基准的空白，解决无标记场景下空间对齐困难的问题。Filling the gap in the UMI-style embodied teaching paradigm regarding the lack of high-precision end-effector localization benchmarks, solving the problem of difficult spatial alignment in markerless scenarios.

生成式 3D 编辑与物理一致性Generative 3D Editing and Physical Consistency

该方向旨在解决生成式 3D 编辑中物理规律缺失与几何一致性差的痛点。核心思路是从纯视觉生成转向“物理感知 + 生成先验”的融合，利用扩散模型学习物理分布或显式约束（如阴影、遮挡、动力学），以替代昂贵的传统物理引擎。演进路径从早期的单图重光照与物体移除（如 ObjectDrop），发展到引入多视图一致性（MVInpainter）和 3D 高斯表示（InFusion），再到构建显式物理约束的世界模型（PhysMani, PhysCtrl），最终追求在具身智能中实现可交互、可预测且符合物理定律的 3D 场景编辑与规划。This direction aims to address the pain points of missing physical laws and poor geometric consistency in generative 3D editing. The core idea is to shift from pure visual generation to a fusion of 'physical perception + generative priors', utilizing diffusion models to learn physical distributions or explicit constraints (such as shadows, occlusions, and dynamics) to replace expensive traditional physics engines. The evolutionary path has progressed from early single-image relighting and object removal (e.g., ObjectDrop), to introducing multi-view consistency (MVInpainter) and 3D Gaussian representations (InFusion), and further to constructing explicit physical constraint world models (PhysMani, PhysCtrl), ultimately pursuing interactive, predictable, and physics-compliant 3D scene editing and planning in embodied intelligence.

方法: Method: 利用MLLM将单目图像转化为可执行代码以重建场景，并引入仿真器内的物理闭环机制让模型自我修正物理错误。Utilizing MLLMs to convert monocular images into executable code for scene reconstruction, and introducing a physical closed-loop mechanism within the simulator to allow the model to self-correct physical errors.
推进: Advance: 首次将“生成即编程”范式引入具身仿真，通过代码约束显式保证了刚体与连杆结构的物理一致性，超越了开环生成的局限。For the first time, introducing the 'generation as programming' paradigm into embodied simulation, explicitly guaranteeing physical consistency of rigid bodies and linkage structures through code constraints, surpassing the limitations of open-loop generation.

动机: Motivation: 单图3D重建常面临几何与纹理不一致问题，且生成的资产缺乏物理碰撞属性，难以直接用于具身交互。Single-image 3D reconstruction often faces issues of geometric and texture inconsistency, and the generated assets lack physical collision attributes, making them difficult to use directly for embodied interaction.
方法: Method: 提出“生成即重建”框架，将旋转编码嵌入Mesh顶点并独立计算平移缩放，结合生成式纹理模块完成场景级重建。Proposing a 'generation as reconstruction' framework that embeds rotation encoding into mesh vertices and independently calculates translation and scaling, combining generative texture modules to complete scene-level reconstruction.
推进: Advance: 实现了场景级指标（CD/IoU）的显著突破，并提供了资产级（Asset-centric）的Mesh输出，为下游物理交互任务提供了更鲁棒的几何基础。Achieving significant breakthroughs in scene-level metrics (CD/IoU) and providing asset-centric mesh outputs, offering a more robust geometric foundation for downstream physical interaction tasks.

动机: Motivation: 揭示当前生成式世界模型研究中“预测误差低”与“实际机器人控制性能高”之间的严重脱节，质疑单纯追求物理预测精度的有效性。Revealing the severe disconnect between 'low prediction error' and 'high actual robot control performance' in current generative world model research, questioning the effectiveness of solely pursuing physical prediction accuracy.
方法: Method: 通过受控的差速机器人实验，对比了离线评估指标（如Rollout误差）与闭环控制表现，并验证了传统轨迹回放RMSE的预测能力。Through controlled differential robot experiments, it compares offline evaluation metrics (e.g., Rollout error) with closed-loop control performance and validates the predictive capability of traditional trajectory replay RMSE.
推进: Advance: 推翻了“预测越准控制越好”的直觉假设，指出评估协议必须模拟真实反馈循环，否则生成式物理模型在具身任务中可能失效。Overturning the intuitive assumption that 'better prediction leads to better control', it points out that evaluation protocols must simulate real feedback loops, otherwise generative physical models may fail in embodied tasks.

Honeycomb: Constant-Size Scene Memory Representation for Video World Models
动机: Motivation: 针对现有视频世界模型因场景记忆无限膨胀导致计算资源浪费及长程一致性下降的问题，旨在探索一种恒定大小的场景记忆表示以维持物理规律下的时空连贯性。Addressing the problem of wasted computational resources and decreased long-range consistency caused by the infinite expansion of scene memory in existing video world models, this work aims to explore a constant-sized scene memory representation to maintain spatiotemporal coherence under physical laws.
方法: Method: 提出Honeycomb架构，利用六个固定平面作为硬约束来压缩和存储场景记忆，通过低秩分解和深度反投影技术替代传统的无限膨胀存储机制。Proposing the Honeycomb architecture, this method utilizes six fixed planes as hard constraints to compress and store scene memory, replacing the traditional infinite expansion storage mechanism with low-rank decomposition and depth back-projection techniques.
推进: Advance: 在回看一致性（Revisit Consistency）指标上显著优于基线，证明了在恒定内存约束下仍能维持有效的物理场景记忆，为具身智能中的长程规划提供了轻量级解决方案。Significantly outperforming baselines on the Revisit Consistency metric, this work proves that effective physical scene memory can be maintained under constant memory constraints, providing a lightweight solution for long-range planning in embodied intelligence.

动机: Motivation: 针对生成式3D编辑中物理规律缺失及传统物理引擎计算昂贵的问题，旨在构建一种能融合物理先验与视觉生成的具身智能框架。Addressing the lack of physical laws in generative 3D editing and the high computational cost of traditional physics engines, aiming to construct an embodied intelligence framework that integrates physical priors with visual generation.
方法: Method: 提出Agentic RSR流水线，利用Codex Agent协同进行3D场景重建与执行代码生成，引入URDF锚定尺度以消除几何漂移，并采用三阶段策略迭代优化。Proposing the Agentic RSR pipeline, utilizing a Codex Agent to collaboratively perform 3D scene reconstruction and code generation, introducing URDF anchoring to eliminate geometric drift, and adopting a three-stage strategy for iterative optimization.
推进: Advance: 通过引入物理锚定与Agent推理机制，成功将真实场景下的任务成功率提升至仿真水平的80%，实现了从纯视觉生成向物理可执行生成的关键跨越。By introducing physical anchoring and agent reasoning mechanisms, successfully increasing the task success rate in real-world scenarios to 80% of simulation levels, achieving a critical leap from pure visual generation to physically executable generation.

触觉感知与多模态融合Tactile perception and multimodal fusion

本方向旨在解决具身智能在接触式操作中因视觉盲区与力控缺失导致的感知失效问题。核心思路是从单一视觉感知向触觉 - 视觉 - 语言多模态融合演进，通过引入触觉传感器数据构建联合表征。演进路径上，早期工作聚焦于低成本仿真（如FOTS）与异构传感器统一表示（如T3），随后转向将触觉信号作为核心模态嵌入VLA架构（如TLA、ForceVLA），解决接触任务中的物理交互理解。近期研究进一步强调触觉与视觉的解耦与语义对齐（如OmniVTLA、Tactile-WAM），并致力于构建高保真、去偏的基准数据集（如RCT、RoboTacDex）以推动模型在真实复杂场景中的泛化能力，最终实现从“看”到“摸”再到“懂”的具身智能闭环。This direction aims to address perception failures in embodied intelligence during contact operations caused by visual blind spots and lack of force control. The core idea is to evolve from single visual perception to tactile-visual-language multimodal fusion, constructing joint representations by introducing tactile sensor data. On the evolutionary path, early work focused on low-cost simulation (e.g., FOTS) and unified representations of heterogeneous sensors (e.g., T3), subsequently shifting to embedding tactile signals as a core modality into VLA architectures (e.g., TLA, ForceVLA) to solve physical interaction understanding in contact tasks. Recent research further emphasizes the decoupling and semantic alignment of tactile and visual modalities (e.g., OmniVTLA, Tactile-WAM), and is committed to building high-fidelity, debiased benchmark datasets (e.g., RCT, RoboTacDex) to promote model generalization in real-world complex scenarios, ultimately achieving an embodied intelligence loop from 'seeing' to 'touching' to 'understanding'.

推进: Advance: 在球放置任务中将成功率从10%提升至66.7%，证明了触觉信号可作为高效的弱监督信号来优化视觉空间注意力，但在软体或复杂场景泛化上仍有局限。Increasing the success rate in ball placement tasks from 10% to 66.7%, proving that tactile signals can serve as efficient weak supervision signals to optimize visual spatial attention, though limitations remain in generalization to soft bodies or complex scenarios.

YUBI-STAG: Contact and Semantic-Rich Alignment for VLAs via Automated Video-Language Grounding
动机: Motivation: 针对现有 VLA 模型缺乏对接触细节（如谁碰了谁、怎么碰）的语义理解，导致物理常识与操作指令脱节的问题。Addressing the problem that existing VLA models lack semantic understanding of contact details (e.g., who touched whom, how they touched), leading to a disconnect between physical common sense and manipulation instructions.
方法: Method: 提出 YUBI-STAG 框架，利用自动化视频 - 语言对齐技术挖掘接触细节，并将其蒸馏为 VLM 的接触感知能力。Proposing the YUBI-STAG framework, utilizing automated video-language alignment technology to mine contact details and distill them into the contact perception capabilities of VLMs.
推进: Advance: 证明了仅通过视觉语义对齐即可让模型习得精细的接触检测能力，为无触觉传感器的具身智能提供了新的语义对齐路径。Demonstrating that models can learn fine-grained contact detection capabilities solely through visual semantic alignment, providing a new semantic alignment path for embodied intelligence without tactile sensors.

遥操作与具身数据合成Teleoperation and Embodied Data Synthesis

该方向旨在通过遥操作与数据合成解决具身感知中数据稀缺与触觉缺失的痛点。核心思路是利用VR/AR设备（如Vision Pro）构建沉浸式遥操作闭环，将人类灵巧操作转化为高质量机器人数据，同时结合合成数据技术（如视频编辑、世界模型）模拟触觉与物理交互，降低实机采集成本。演进路径从早期依赖昂贵硬件的视觉遥操作，转向融合低成本触觉反馈、利用人类视频“白嫖”策略、以及通过合成数据填补Sim-to-Real鸿沟，最终实现无需机器人数据即可训练具备精细触觉与操作能力的智能体。This direction aims to address data scarcity and tactile absence in embodied perception through teleoperation and data synthesis. The core idea is to utilize VR/AR devices (e.g., Vision Pro) to build an immersive teleoperation loop, converting human dexterous manipulation into high-quality robot data, while combining synthetic data techniques (e.g., video editing, world models) to simulate tactile and physical interactions, reducing real-world data collection costs. The evolution path shifts from early visual teleoperation relying on expensive hardware to integrating low-cost tactile feedback, leveraging human videos to 'freeload' policies, and using synthetic data to bridge the Sim-to-Real gap, ultimately enabling agents with fine tactile and manipulation capabilities to be trained without robot data.

方法: Method: 提出Action Map Policy，将高维连续动作映射为像素级热力图，通过图像分割任务替代传统的动作回归或扩散生成，利用多视角融合实现空间对齐。Proposing Action Map Policy, mapping high-dimensional continuous actions to pixel-level heatmaps, replacing traditional action regression or diffusion generation with image segmentation tasks, and achieving spatial alignment through multi-view fusion.
推进: Advance: 实现了13毫秒的超低延迟推理，显著优于扩散策略，证明了像素级分类在特定遥操作闭环中可替代复杂生成模型，但同时也揭示了其对相机标定精度的极高敏感性。Achieving ultra-low latency inference of 13 milliseconds, significantly outperforming diffusion policies, proving that pixel-level classification can replace complex generative models in specific teleoperation closed-loops, but also revealing its extreme sensitivity to camera calibration accuracy.

FPicker: Topology-Guided Evolution for Filament Tracing in Low-SNR Microscopy
动机: Motivation: 针对低信噪比显微成像中传统方法依赖边界框导致漂移、且难以处理开曲线拓扑断裂的问题，旨在探索不依赖海量标注数据、利用物理几何先验解决感知难题的路径。Addressing the issues in low signal-to-noise ratio microscopic imaging where traditional methods rely on bounding boxes leading to drift and struggle with open-curve topological breaks, aiming to explore a path that solves perception challenges using physical geometric priors without relying on massive annotated data.
方法: Method: 提出FPicker算法，摒弃易漂移的闭合边界框，采用中心点加端点向量的开曲线表示，并通过拓扑引导的演化机制在极端噪声下追踪丝状结构。Proposing the FPicker algorithm, which abandons drift-prone closed bounding boxes in favor of an open-curve representation using center points and endpoint vectors, tracking filamentous structures via a topology-guided evolution mechanism under extreme noise.
推进: Advance: 证明了在低信噪比环境下，基于几何约束的物理先验比单纯的数据驱动暴力堆叠更可靠，且其Sim-to-Real迁移效果验证了合成数据在构建鲁棒感知模型中的有效性。Demonstrating that in low signal-to-noise ratio environments, physical priors based on geometric constraints are more reliable than simple data-driven brute force stacking, and its Sim-to-Real transfer effects validate the effectiveness of synthetic data in building robust perception models.

动机: Motivation: 针对水下机器人感知中真实数据稀缺且采集成本高昂的痛点，试图利用合成数据填补数据荒。Addressing the pain points of scarce real data and high acquisition costs in underwater robot perception, it attempts to fill the data void using synthetic data.
方法: Method: 构建OceanSim仿真平台并引入SDG管道，生成带标签的海胆检测数据，支持ROS2集成及环境参数（如浑浊度）调节。Constructed the OceanSim simulation platform and introduced an SDG pipeline to generate labeled sea urchin detection data, supporting ROS2 integration and environmental parameter (e.g., turbidity) adjustment.
推进: Advance: 开源了可扩展的合成数据生成工具链，但实验揭示了仅靠数据堆砌无法解决水下光学退化导致的Sim-to-Real鸿沟，强调了渲染逼真度与物理建模的重要性。Open-sourced an extensible synthetic data generation toolchain, but experiments revealed that merely stacking data cannot solve the Sim-to-Real gap caused by underwater optical degradation, emphasizing the importance of rendering fidelity and physical modeling.

DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决机器人触觉数据稀缺问题，利用人类触觉数据作为低成本“平替”来训练机器人世界模型。Aiming to address the scarcity of robot tactile data, it utilizes human tactile data as a low-cost 'substitute' to train robot world models.
方法: Method: 通过对齐人与机器人手套的布局及动作空间重定向，将人类触觉交互数据转化为机器人世界的监督信号，并探索多种合成数据生成策略。By aligning human and robot glove layouts and performing action space remapping, human tactile interaction data is converted into supervision signals for the robot world, exploring various synthetic data generation strategies.
推进: Advance: 验证了引入人类触觉数据能显著提升机器人对接触动态的预测能力，但同时也发现合成策略效果参差不齐，表明跨任务迁移与人类模式泛化仍存在不确定性。Validated that introducing human tactile data can significantly improve the robot's ability to predict contact dynamics, but also found that synthetic strategy effects vary, indicating uncertainty in cross-task transfer and human pattern generalization.

World Observer: Joint Actor-Observer Generation for Persistent World Modeling
动机: Motivation: 针对传统世界模型在物体移出视野后产生幻觉或遗忘的痛点，解决遥操作与数据合成中因视角受限导致的感知不连续问题。Addresses the pain point of traditional world models generating hallucinations or forgetting objects after they leave the field of view, solving the perception discontinuity caused by limited viewing angles in teleoperation and data synthesis.
方法: Method: 提出双视角架构，由Actor负责当前视野观测，多组Observer协同监控盲区，结合高分辨率参考图与Observer Sink机制构建持久化世界模型。Proposes a dual-view architecture where the Actor is responsible for current field-of-view observations, while multiple groups of Observers collaboratively monitor blind spots, combining high-resolution reference maps with the Observer Sink mechanism to construct a persistent world model.
推进: Advance: 突破了单一视角依赖，实现了物体在遮挡或移出视野后的状态保持，为利用合成数据构建具备长程记忆与物理一致性的遥操作闭环提供了新范式。Breaks through single-view dependency, achieving state retention for objects after occlusion or leaving the field of view, providing a new paradigm for building teleoperation loops with long-range memory and physical consistency using synthetic data.

Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching
动机: Motivation: 针对UMI类遥操作教学中末端定位精度不足导致抓取失败的问题，以及现有数据集缺乏操作SLAM基准的空白。Addressing the problem of grasping failures due to insufficient end-effector localization accuracy in UMI-style teleoperation teaching, as well as the gap in existing datasets lacking manipulation SLAM benchmarks.

长视频理解与空间记忆基准Long video understanding and spatial memory benchmarks

本角度聚焦于具身智能在长视频理解与空间记忆基准下的感知演进。核心动机在于解决现有模型在长时序场景下“看多忘多”、空间推理断裂及缺乏物理常识的顽疾。工作从单纯延长上下文长度（如LVBench），转向引入空间超感（Cambrian-S）、几何一致性（WorldPlay）及因果推理（OWMDrive）等机制。演进路径表现为：从被动接收视频流到主动构建世界模型，从依赖全量计算到采用“提议-验证”等高效策略（TimeProVe），并逐步融合触觉、物理先验及安全诊断（EgoSafetyBench），旨在构建具备类人时空记忆与鲁棒感知能力的具身系统。This perspective focuses on the evolution of perception in embodied intelligence under long video understanding and spatial memory benchmarks. The core motivation is to address the persistent issues of existing models, such as 'seeing much but forgetting much' in long temporal scenarios, broken spatial reasoning, and lack of physical common sense. Work has shifted from simply extending context length (e.g., LVBench) to introducing mechanisms like spatial supersensing (Cambrian-S), geometric consistency (WorldPlay), and causal reasoning (OWMDrive). The evolutionary path manifests as: from passively receiving video streams to actively constructing world models, from relying on full-scale computation to adopting efficient strategies like 'propose-verify' (TimeProVe), and gradually integrating tactile, physical priors, and safety diagnostics (EgoSafetyBench), aiming to build embodied systems with human-like spatiotemporal memory and robust perception capabilities.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有基准测试视频过短，无法评估模型在长时序下的时空推理与记忆能力。Address the issue that existing benchmarks use videos that are too short to evaluate models' spatiotemporal reasoning and memory capabilities in long temporal sequences.
方法: Method: 构建LVBench，包含平均4101秒的超长视频，并实施严格的线索时长标注与去文本化过滤。Construct LVBench, containing ultra-long videos averaging 4101 seconds, and implement strict clue duration annotation and text-free filtering.
推进: Advance: 揭示了大模型在长视频理解上的巨大鸿沟，迫使研究关注真正的长时序记忆而非短时上下文拟合。Reveal the huge gap in large models' long video understanding, forcing research to focus on true long-term memory rather than short-context fitting.

TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living
动机: Motivation: 解决长视频VLM全量编码计算成本过高、推理效率低下的问题。Address the excessively high computational cost of full encoding and low inference efficiency of long video VLMs.

推进: Advance: 在保持精度的前提下将计算成本降低93%，证明了高效长时序推理可通过分层策略实现，但依赖检测器的召回率。Reducing computational cost by 93% while maintaining accuracy demonstrates that efficient long-horizon reasoning can be achieved via hierarchical strategies, yet it relies on the recall rate of detectors.

推进: Advance: 在无需额外训练的情况下实现了高准确率的异常帧检测，为从长视频中提取隐性操作知识提供了新范式。Achieves high-accuracy anomalous frame detection without additional training, providing a new paradigm for extracting implicit operational knowledge from long videos.

iSEE: Object Permanence Through Self-Supervision
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能在长时序遮挡场景下物体恒常性缺失及“看多忘多”的顽疾，旨在解决自监督Slot模型在物体消失后无法维持表征的问题。Addresses the lack of object constancy and the chronic issue of "seeing much, forgetting much" in embodied agents under long-horizon occlusion scenarios, aiming to solve the problem where self-supervised Slot models fail to maintain representations after objects disappear.

推进: Advance: 在无标签条件下实现了接近有监督水平的物体恒常性，证明了自监督机制下长时序空间记忆构建的可行性，为规划提供了更鲁棒的动作信号。Achieved object constancy approaching supervised levels under label-free conditions, proving the feasibility of constructing long-horizon spatial memory under self-supervised mechanisms and providing more robust action signals for planning.

触觉神经形态与边缘计算Tactile Neuromorphic and Edge Computing

本方向聚焦于解决边缘机器人触觉感知中实时性与能耗的矛盾，核心思路是将神经形态事件流处理与触觉传感深度耦合，实现片上感知 - 计算一体化。演进路径从早期依赖离线训练和离散化处理的工程集成（如GelNeuro），逐步向真正的异步事件驱动、脉冲神经网络（SNN）原生推理及自适应在线学习转变。通过消除传统架构中频繁的数据搬运瓶颈，利用事件流的稀疏性大幅降低功耗，使机器人能在资源受限的端侧实现毫秒级纹理识别与精细操作，推动触觉系统从‘被动采集’向‘主动感知’的神经形态范式跨越。This direction focuses on resolving the contradiction between real-time performance and energy consumption in edge robot tactile perception. The core strategy involves deeply coupling neuromorphic event stream processing with tactile sensing to achieve on-chip perception-computation integration. The evolution path progresses from early engineering integration relying on offline training and discrete processing (e.g., GelNeuro) towards true asynchronous event-driven, native Spiking Neural Network (SNN) inference, and adaptive online learning. By eliminating the frequent data movement bottlenecks in traditional architectures and leveraging the sparsity of event streams to significantly reduce power consumption, robots can achieve millisecond-level texture recognition and fine manipulation on resource-constrained edge devices, driving tactile systems to cross from a 'passive acquisition' paradigm to a 'active perception' neuromorphic paradigm.

触觉数据合成与物理仿真Tactile Data Synthesis and Physical Simulation

该方向旨在通过高保真触觉数据合成与物理仿真，弥合Sim2Real中接触物理的鸿沟。核心思路是从依赖有限元（FEM）的刚性/弹性体物理建模，转向利用大规模多视图数据驱动的可变形世界模型。演进路径表现为：早期工作聚焦于基于FEM的精确物理协议以解决弹性体变形问题，但受限于计算开销与传感器特异性；近期工作如Deform360则转向构建海量真实多模态数据，试图用数据驱动弥补显式物理模型的泛化不足，尽管仍面临触觉模态单一（缺乏切向力）及实时性挑战，标志着从纯物理仿真向“数据+物理”混合范式的过渡。This direction aims to bridge the gap in contact physics in Sim2Real through high-fidelity tactile data synthesis and physical simulation. The core strategy shifts from relying on Finite Element Method (FEM) rigid/elastic body physical modeling to utilizing large-scale multi-view data-driven deformable world models. The evolution path is characterized by: early work focusing on precise physical protocols based on FEM to solve elastic body deformation problems, but limited by computational overhead and sensor specificity; recent work like Deform360 shifts towards building massive real-world multi-modal data, attempting to compensate for the generalization insufficiency of explicit physical models with data-driven approaches, although still facing challenges of single tactile modality (lack of tangential force) and real-time performance, marking the transition from pure physical simulation to a 'data + physics' hybrid paradigm.

方法: Method: 构建双臂异构机器人系统，通过大量离线校准与特定硬件（Elfin+UR5）实现基于触觉与音频反馈的闭环控制，精确建模琴弦与手指的接触物理。Constructing a dual-arm heterogeneous robot system, achieving closed-loop control based on tactile and audio feedback through extensive offline calibration and specific hardware (Elfin+UR5), precisely modeling the contact physics between strings and fingers.
推进: Advance: 在填补从单音到乐句的接触物理控制空白上取得突破，验证了多模态触觉反馈对复杂弹性体交互时序控制的有效性，但受限于特定硬件配置导致泛化性不足。Achieving a breakthrough in filling the gap in contact physics control from single notes to musical phrases, validating the effectiveness of multimodal tactile feedback for temporal control of complex elastomer interactions, though generalization is limited by specific hardware configurations.

General-Purpose Sim2Real Protocol for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation With Marker-Based Visuotactile Sensors
动机: Motivation: 传统刚性体仿真无法准确模拟接触中的弹性变形，导致Sim2Real迁移失败，需回归物理本质解决接触丰富任务。Traditional rigid body simulation cannot accurately simulate elastic deformation during contact, leading to Sim2Real transfer failure, requiring a return to physical essence to solve contact-rich tasks.
方法: Method: 提出基于Marker传感器的Sim2Real协议，采用相互穿透约束（IPC）模拟弹性体变形，结合自监督预训练优化RL策略。Proposing a Sim2Real protocol based on Marker sensors, employing Interpenetration Constraints (IPC) to simulate elastic body deformation, combined with self-supervised pre-training to optimize RL policies.
推进: Advance: 验证了FEM与IPC在弹性体变形模拟中的有效性，显著提升了未见任务的泛化能力，但受限于专用传感器与计算实时性瓶颈。Validated the effectiveness of FEM and IPC in simulating elastomer deformation, significantly improving generalization capabilities for unseen tasks, yet constrained by dedicated sensors and computational real-time bottlenecks.

用户感知与评估基准User Perception and Evaluation Benchmarks

本方向聚焦于将感知系统的评估标准从传统的机器指标（如mAP、成功率）转向用户的主观感知与信任度。早期工作多依赖客观数据，忽视了人类对机器人行为流畅度、安全性及自然度的真实体验。随着多模态感知技术（如3D视觉、触觉）的成熟，研究重心开始转向量化“技术提升”如何转化为“用户感知”。核心思路是通过受控的用户研究，建立客观性能与主观评价之间的映射关系，揭示当前系统在达到高成功率后仍存在的信任缺口，从而指导未来感知算法在真实部署中兼顾效率与用户体验。This direction focuses on shifting evaluation standards for perception systems from traditional machine metrics (such as mAP and success rates) to user subjective perception and trust. Early work relied heavily on objective data, overlooking the human experience of robot behavior fluency, safety, and naturalness. With the maturation of multimodal perception technologies (such as 3D vision and touch), the research focus has shifted towards quantifying how "technical improvements" translate into "user perception." The core approach involves conducting controlled user studies to establish a mapping between objective performance and subjective evaluation, revealing the trust gap that persists even after systems achieve high success rates, thereby guiding future perception algorithms to balance efficiency and user experience in real-world deployments.

方法: Method: 提出Best-of-Evidence (BoE)框架，利用部分验证机制计算证据价值，在候选池质量受限的情况下结构化地选择最可靠的感知结果。Proposing the Best-of-Evidence (BoE) framework, utilizing partial verification mechanisms to calculate evidence value, and structurally selecting the most reliable perception results when candidate pool quality is limited.

方法: Method: 提出ActiveArena基准，引入可动头颈机制强制机器人进行主动搜索，对比不同记忆采样策略（分块采样vs子任务触发）在ID与OOD场景下的表现。Propose the ActiveArena benchmark, introducing a movable head-neck mechanism to force robots into active search, and comparing the performance of different memory sampling strategies (block sampling vs. subtask triggering) under ID and OOD scenarios.
推进: Advance: 填补了主动感知基准空白，量化了ID-OOD差距并揭示简单记忆策略优于复杂架构的真相，证明了动态环境下的主动感知是提升用户信任的关键而非被动接收。Fill the gap in active perception benchmarks, quantify the ID-OOD gap, and reveal the truth that simple memory strategies outperform complex architectures, proving that active perception in dynamic environments is key to enhancing user trust rather than passive reception.

视觉里程计与压缩感知Visual Odometry and Compressed Sensing

在视觉里程计与压缩感知交叉领域，核心动机在于解决高带宽视频传输与存储成本之间的矛盾，同时利用现有压缩视频流中的隐含信息。传统SLAM系统依赖原始像素，而本方向试图挖掘H.264/H.265等编码标准中运动矢量（Motion Vectors）等压缩副产品。演进路径从简单的运动矢量提取，发展为利用编码器残差或特定编码结构来初始化或优化光流跟踪。VOCA等研究证明了在极高压缩比下，利用解码器输出的运动信息仍能维持里程计精度，标志着从“丢弃压缩数据”向“利用压缩数据”的范式转变，虽理论深度有限，但极大提升了工程实用性。In the intersection of visual odometry and compressed sensing, the core motivation lies in resolving the contradiction between high-bandwidth video transmission and storage costs, while leveraging implicit information within existing compressed video streams. Traditional SLAM systems rely on raw pixels, whereas this direction attempts to mine compression side-products such as Motion Vectors in coding standards like H.264/H.265. The evolutionary path has progressed from simple motion vector extraction to utilizing encoder residuals or specific coding structures to initialize or optimize optical flow tracking. Research such as VOCA has demonstrated that motion information from decoder outputs can maintain odometry accuracy even at extremely high compression ratios, marking a paradigm shift from "discarding compressed data" to "utilizing compressed data." Although theoretical depth is limited, it has significantly enhanced engineering practicality.

视觉强化学习特征解耦Visual Reinforcement Learning Feature Disentanglement

在视觉强化学习感知方向，核心痛点在于环境中的无关视觉噪声严重阻碍策略泛化。本角度聚焦于特征解耦，旨在将任务相关语义与无关背景/纹理分离。演进路径从早期的简单数据增强，转向显式的解耦架构设计，如通过动态预测约束或随机扰动强制模型学习不变性。最新工作如#104，进一步结合自监督预训练与双分支EMA结构，试图在复杂物理环境中实现鲁棒的任务表征，虽理论假设仍待验证，但为提升VRL在未见场景下的适应性提供了工程化新思路。In the domain of visual reinforcement learning perception, the core pain point is that irrelevant visual noise in the environment severely hinders policy generalization. This perspective focuses on feature disentanglement, aiming to separate task-relevant semantics from irrelevant backgrounds/textures. The evolutionary path has shifted from early simple data augmentation to explicit disentanglement architecture design, such as using dynamic prediction constraints or random perturbations to force models to learn invariance. Recent work, such as #104, further combines self-supervised pre-training with a dual-branch EMA structure, attempting to achieve robust task representation in complex physical environments. Although theoretical assumptions remain to be verified, it provides a new engineering-oriented approach to improve VRL adaptability in unseen scenarios.

触觉反馈与幻觉综述Tactile Feedback and Illusion Review

本角度聚焦于具身智能中触觉感知的“幻觉”机制，旨在解决高保真触觉硬件成本高昂且难以微型化的痛点。核心思路是从“物理模拟”转向“感知欺骗”，利用多感官整合（如视觉-触觉耦合）和自适应算法，在低分辨率硬件上通过心理物理学原理诱导大脑产生逼真的触觉体验。演进路径从早期依赖视觉欺骗的伪触觉清单，发展为结合柔性电子与自适应算法的硬件平台，最终指向构建无需复杂物理引擎即可实现丰富触觉交互的具身智能系统。This perspective focuses on the "illusion" mechanism of tactile perception in embodied intelligence, aiming to address the pain point of high costs and difficulty in miniaturization for high-fidelity tactile hardware. The core approach shifts from "physical simulation" to "perceptual deception," utilizing multisensory integration (such as visual-tactile coupling) and adaptive algorithms to induce the brain to generate realistic tactile experiences on low-resolution hardware through psychophysical principles. The evolutionary path has progressed from early pseudo-tactile lists relying on visual deception to hardware platforms combining flexible electronics with adaptive algorithms, ultimately pointing towards embodied intelligent systems capable of rich tactile interaction without complex physical engines.

机器人坐标系与特征对齐Robot Coordinate System and Feature Alignment

本方向聚焦于解决机器人感知与动作空间间的坐标系错位及特征解耦难题。核心思路是将视觉输入从相机视角强制映射至机器人基座视角，消除因视角变化导致的动作策略失效。演进路径从依赖多视角记忆或笨重的点云编码，转向构建直接兼容2D预训练架构的机器人中心特征图。通过保留网格结构并内嵌3D坐标，实现了视觉特征与动作空间的无缝对齐，显著提升了模型在动态视角下的泛化能力与精度，但同时也对传感器标定提出了更高要求。This direction focuses on solving the challenges of coordinate system misalignment and feature decoupling between robot perception and action spaces. The core idea is to forcibly map visual inputs from the camera perspective to the robot base perspective, eliminating action strategy failures caused by viewpoint changes. The evolutionary path shifts from relying on multi-view memory or cumbersome point cloud encoding to constructing robot-centric feature maps directly compatible with 2D pre-trained architectures. By retaining the grid structure and embedding 3D coordinates, seamless alignment between visual features and action spaces is achieved, significantly improving the model's generalization ability and accuracy under dynamic viewpoints, while also imposing higher requirements on sensor calibration.

具身长程记忆与可验证规划Embodied Long-Range Memory and Verifiable Planning

本角度聚焦于解决具身智能在长程任务中的状态遗忘与规划一致性问题，核心思路是通过引入可验证机制与固化状态记忆，打破传统模型“金鱼记忆”的局限。演进路径从单纯依赖大模型的概率生成，转向构建显式的物体状态Token（如POT）以维持物理世界的一致性，并引入部分验证策略（如BoE）在候选规划中筛选最优解。尽管当前方法多依赖几何谓词或外部感知模块，属于工程化“缝补”，但已显著提升了长程任务的鲁棒性，为从感知到规划的可信闭环奠定了基础。This angle focuses on solving state forgetting and planning consistency issues in embodied intelligence during long-range tasks. The core idea is to break the limitations of traditional models' 'goldfish memory' by introducing verifiable mechanisms and solidified state memory. The evolutionary path shifts from solely relying on large model probabilistic generation to constructing explicit object state Tokens (such as POT) to maintain physical world consistency, and introducing partial verification strategies (such as BoE) to select optimal solutions from candidate plans. Although current methods mostly rely on geometric predicates or external perception modules, representing engineering 'patches', they have significantly improved the robustness of long-range tasks, laying the foundation for a trustworthy closed loop from perception to planning.

透明化决策与概念解释Transparent decision-making and conceptual explanation

本角度聚焦于解决Embodied AI中感知与决策的“黑盒”困境，核心思路是将不可解释的视觉特征转化为人类可理解的概念逻辑或几何约束。演进路径从单纯依赖VLM的语义标注（如#12），转向结合物理仿真与几何解析的混合验证机制（如#13）。前者通过概念树实现逻辑干预，后者利用VLM作为裁判优化物理一致性，共同推动系统从“能执行”向“可解释、可干预、物理可信”的透明化决策方向发展，旨在建立人类与机器人之间基于逻辑与物理双重验证的信任桥梁。This perspective focuses on resolving the 'black box' dilemma of perception and decision-making in Embodied AI, with the core approach transforming uninterpretable visual features into human-understandable conceptual logic or geometric constraints. The evolutionary path shifts from relying solely on VLM-based semantic annotation (e.g., #12) to hybrid verification mechanisms combining physical simulation and geometric analysis (e.g., #13). The former achieves logical intervention via concept trees, while the latter utilizes VLMs as referees to optimize physical consistency, collectively driving the system towards transparent decision-making that is 'interpretable, intervenable, and physically credible' from merely 'executable', aiming to establish a trust bridge between humans and robots based on dual verification of logic and physics.

CLAMP: Constrained Decoding for Vision-Language Embodied Planning
动机: Motivation: 针对视觉语言模型在具身规划中产生的幻觉问题，旨在通过外部约束机制将不可靠的语义生成强行拉回物理现实，解决“想当然”导致的决策黑盒风险。Addressing hallucination issues in embodied planning by visual language models, this work aims to forcibly pull unreliable semantic generation back to physical reality through external constraint mechanisms, mitigating the black-box decision risks caused by 'assumptions'.

感知鲁棒性与对抗防御Perception Robustness and Adversarial Defense

本方向聚焦于具身智能在感知层面的鲁棒性与对抗防御，旨在解决传感器故障、恶劣环境及物理对抗攻击导致的系统失效。核心思路从单一模态的容错训练转向多模态互补融合，并引入物理约束与注意力机制防御。演进路径上，早期工作侧重于通过数据增强应对传感器缺失（如#8），中期开始关注合成数据驱动的恶劣天气去噪（如#25），近期则深入至对抗样本的物理攻击与防御博弈，强调在真实物理交互中保护视觉注意力不被劫持（如#16, #39），标志着从被动适应向主动防御的范式转变。This direction focuses on the robustness and adversarial defense of embodied intelligence at the perception level, aiming to address system failures caused by sensor malfunctions, harsh environments, and physical adversarial attacks. The core approach shifts from fault-tolerant training in single modalities to multi-modal complementary fusion, introducing physical constraints and attention mechanisms for defense. In terms of evolution, early work focused on handling sensor loss through data augmentation (e.g., #8), mid-stage work began focusing on harsh weather denoising driven by synthetic data (e.g., #25), and recent efforts delve into the physical attack and defense game of adversarial samples, emphasizing protecting visual attention from hijacking in real physical interactions (e.g., #16, #39), marking a paradigm shift from passive adaptation to active defense.

软硬协同与物理辅助感知Hardware-Software Co-Design and Physics-Assisted Perception

软硬协同与物理辅助感知旨在打破纯算法依赖，通过定制硬件结构与物理机制降低感知与控制的复杂度。该方向演进逻辑从单纯追求算法泛化转向“物理先验引导”，利用几何约束、触觉反馈或因果闭环将物理规律内嵌于系统设计中。早期工作多关注单一物理辅助，现发展为算法与硬件的深度耦合，如TCAM利用因果分析优化硬件策略，或Fabric系统通过CAD形状先验辅助分割。核心在于以物理实体为“锚点”，减少数据需求并提升在动态、非结构化环境中的鲁棒性，实现从“试错学习”到“物理引导的高效感知”的范式转变。Hardware-Software Co-Design and Physics-Assisted Perception aims to break pure algorithmic dependency by customizing hardware structures and physical mechanisms to reduce the complexity of perception and control. The evolutionary logic of this direction shifts from solely pursuing algorithmic generalization to 'physics-prior guidance', embedding physical laws into system design through geometric constraints, tactile feedback, or causal loops. Early work focused mainly on single physical assistance, evolving into deep coupling of algorithms and hardware, such as TCAM using causal analysis to optimize hardware strategies, or the Fabric system assisting segmentation with CAD shape priors. The core lies in using physical entities as 'anchors' to reduce data requirements and enhance robustness in dynamic, unstructured environments, achieving a paradigm shift from 'trial-and-error learning' to 'physics-guided efficient perception'.

方法: Method: 提出双变分自编码器框架，利用混合迁移学习策略，在冻结解码器的前提下微调编码器以生成保留语义一致性的伪数据，并在树莓派等嵌入式硬件上部署。Proposes a dual variational autoencoder framework that utilizes a mixed transfer learning strategy to fine-tune the encoder while freezing the decoder to generate pseudo-data with preserved semantic consistency, and deploys it on embedded hardware such as Raspberry Pi.
推进: Advance: 实现了在资源受限硬件上91%的导航成功率，验证了通过物理约束（如几何先验隐含在仿真中）引导的轻量级感知架构在静态环境下的工程可行性。Achieves a 91% navigation success rate on resource-constrained hardware, validating the engineering feasibility of a lightweight perception architecture guided by physical constraints (such as geometric priors implicitly embedded in simulation) in static environments.

触觉硬件与耐用性工程Tactile Hardware and Durability Engineering

本角度聚焦触觉感知的硬件落地瓶颈，核心思路是从实验室原型转向工程化耐用与低成本方案。演进路径上，早期研究追求高分辨率成像，但忽视了物理脆弱性与高昂成本；近期工作转向结构优化与材料创新，如引入可替换保护膜提升鲁棒性，或摒弃昂贵相机采用分布式色彩传感以降低成本。同时，气动触觉从开环脉冲向闭环双向交互演进，解决了保压与延迟难题。这些进展共同推动了触觉硬件从‘易碎玩具’向‘工业级耐用设备’及‘普惠型低成本终端’的跨越，平衡了性能、寿命与成本。This perspective focuses on the hardware implementation bottlenecks of tactile perception, with the core idea shifting from laboratory prototypes to engineering-grade durability and low-cost solutions. In the evolutionary path, early research pursued high-resolution imaging but neglected physical fragility and high costs; recent work has shifted towards structural optimization and material innovation, such as introducing replaceable protective films to enhance robustness or abandoning expensive cameras in favor of distributed color sensing to reduce costs. Meanwhile, pneumatic tactile systems have evolved from open-loop pulses to closed-loop bidirectional interaction, solving the problems of pressure maintenance and latency. These advancements collectively drive the leap of tactile hardware from 'fragile toys' to 'industrial-grade durable equipment' and 'inclusive low-cost terminals', balancing performance, lifespan, and cost.

2026-08
SpectraTac: A Compact Camera-Free Optical Tactile Sensor with Distributed Color Sensing
动机: Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器（如GelSight）依赖昂贵相机与高算力，难以在低成本场景普及，且带宽与计算资源消耗过大。Existing visual-tactile sensors (e.g., GelSight) rely on expensive cameras and high computing power, making them difficult to popularize in low-cost scenarios, while consuming excessive bandwidth and computing resources.
方法: Method: 提出SpectraTac架构，摒弃传统相机，仅利用三个彩色传感器配合RGB光源，通过分布式颜色传感直接解算力分布。We propose the SpectraTac architecture, which abandons traditional cameras, utilizing only three color sensors paired with RGB light sources to directly distribute computation via distributed color sensing.
推进: Advance: 将硬件成本压缩至5美元以内并显著降低算力需求，虽牺牲分辨率至9区域，但为低成本抓取提供了务实的替代方案。This compresses hardware costs to under $5 and significantly reduces computing power requirements; although resolution is sacrificed to 9 regions, it provides a pragmatic alternative for low-cost grasping.
2026-08
A Durable Vision-Based Tactile Fingertip for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 主流视觉触觉传感器（如DIGIT）物理结构脆弱，易受磨损损坏，缺乏适应真实工业环境的耐用性设计。Mainstream visual-tactile sensors (e.g., DIGIT) have fragile physical structures, are prone to wear and damage, and lack durability designs adapted to real industrial environments.
方法: Method: 设计TPU保护膜与可替换卡槽结构，在不改变核心成像原理的前提下，通过物理防护与模块化更换机制提升硬件寿命。We design TPU protective films and replaceable slot structures to enhance hardware lifespan through physical protection and modular replacement mechanisms without altering the core imaging principle.
推进: Advance: 将传感器耐用性提升两个数量级，验证了工程化防护对延长触觉设备服役周期的关键作用，解决了频繁更换的痛点。This improves sensor durability by two orders of magnitude, validating the critical role of engineering protection in extending the service life of tactile devices and solving the pain point of frequent replacements.
2026-09
A Wearable Pneumatic Device for Continuous, Closed-Loop, Bidirectional Tactile Interaction
动机: Motivation: 传统气动触觉设备多为开环脉冲式，难以维持稳定压力，且缺乏双向交互能力，限制了遥操作体验与闭环控制。Traditional pneumatic tactile devices are mostly open-loop pulse-based, making it difficult to maintain stable pressure, and lack bidirectional interaction capabilities, limiting teleoperation experience and closed-loop control.
方法: Method: 开发可穿戴气动装置，引入闭环控制算法解决保压难题，并设计双向力反馈机制实现机器人抓力到用户手指的实时映射。We develop wearable pneumatic devices, introducing closed-loop control algorithms to solve pressure maintenance issues and designing bidirectional force feedback mechanisms to achieve real-time mapping of robot grip force to user fingers.
推进: Advance: 实现了30Hz以上带宽的稳定闭环双向交互，将气动触觉从实验室演示转化为具备实用价值的可穿戴遥操作硬件。This achieves stable closed-loop bidirectional interaction with bandwidth above 30Hz, transforming pneumatic tactile technology from laboratory demonstrations into wearable teleoperation hardware with practical value.

Hall Effect-Based Tactile Force Detection Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery
动机: Motivation: 针对手术机器人辅助微创手术中触觉感知缺失及现有传感器难以兼顾剪切力检测与小型化的问题，旨在解决硬件在生物环境下的集成痛点。Aiming to address the lack of haptic perception in surgical robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery and the difficulty of existing sensors in balancing shear force detection with miniaturization, this work targets the integration challenges of hardware in biological environments.
方法: Method: 设计并集成毫米级霍尔效应传感器，利用其检测剪切力，并通过神经网络算法对传感器数据进行校准以补偿非线性误差。Designed and integrated millimeter-scale Hall effect sensors to detect shear forces, employing neural network algorithms to calibrate sensor data and compensate for non-linear errors.
推进: Advance: 实现了在微小空间内对剪切力的精确检测，为防打滑提供了关键硬件支持，但受限于生物相容性与临床灭菌要求，尚未完全跨越从实验室原型到临床工程的鸿沟。Achieved precise shear force detection within confined spaces, providing critical hardware support for anti-slip control, yet remains constrained by biocompatibility and clinical sterilization requirements, failing to fully bridge the gap from laboratory prototypes to clinical engineering.

几何驱动的世界模型与推理Geometry-Driven World Models and Reasoning

本角度聚焦于利用显式几何约束（如点云、3D高斯）替代纯像素生成来构建具身世界模型，旨在解决传统视频预测模型缺乏物理一致性与深度理解的问题。核心思路是将几何演化作为推理的中间表示，通过逆动力学或知识蒸馏，让模型从“画视频”转向“推演空间状态”。演进路径从利用外部几何模型辅助训练（如GaussianWAM），转向直接以几何预测为核心构建世界模型（如GeoWAM），并进一步结合逻辑驱动与仿真评估（如VGEBench）以验证长程探索能力，逐步从数据增强迈向具备物理鲁棒性的具身推理。This perspective focuses on constructing embodied world models by replacing pure pixel generation with explicit geometric constraints (e.g., point clouds, 3D Gaussians), aiming to solve the lack of physical consistency and depth understanding in traditional video prediction models. The core idea is to treat geometric evolution as an intermediate representation for reasoning, using inverse dynamics or knowledge distillation to shift models from 'drawing videos' to 'deducing spatial states'. The evolutionary path moves from using external geometric models to assist training (e.g., GaussianWAM), to directly building world models with geometric prediction as the core (e.g., GeoWAM), and further combining logic-driven and simulation-based evaluation (e.g., VGEBench) to verify long-range exploration capabilities, gradually advancing from data augmentation to embodied reasoning with physical robustness.

2026-08
GaussianWAM: Distilling Geometry and Semantics from 3D Gaussian Fields into World-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决世界模型在推理阶段缺乏显式3D理解且视频生成模型物理一致性差的问题，试图在保持推理效率的同时注入几何先验。This aims to address the lack of explicit 3D understanding in world models during the reasoning phase and the poor physical consistency of video generation models, attempting to inject geometric priors while maintaining reasoning efficiency.
方法: Method: 利用3D高斯场作为临时教师，将几何与语义知识蒸馏至世界动作模型（WAM）的潜空间，并在训练阶段强制注入3D约束。We utilize 3D Gaussian fields as temporary teachers to distill geometric and semantic knowledge into the latent space of World Action Models (WAM), and enforce 3D constraints during the training phase.
推进: Advance: 实现了推理零开销且显著提升了LIBERO-Plus等基准的性能，证明了利用外部几何模型进行知识蒸馏是平衡效率与精度的有效策略。This achieves zero overhead during reasoning and significantly improves performance on benchmarks such as LIBERO-Plus, proving that knowledge distillation using external geometric models is an effective strategy for balancing efficiency and accuracy.
2026-08
GeoWAM: Visual Geometry World Action Models for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界模型沉迷于生成逼真但物理意义模糊的像素视频，试图通过回归几何本质来构建更鲁棒的自动驾驶感知与决策模型。Addressing the tendency of existing world models to become obsessed with generating realistic but physically ambiguous pixel videos, this work attempts to build more robust autonomous driving perception and decision models by returning to geometric essence.
方法: Method: 摒弃视频生成，直接构建基于点云预测的世界模型，利用逆动力学设计从几何演化反推自车运动轨迹。Abandoning video generation, we directly construct a world model based on point cloud prediction, using inverse dynamics design to infer the ego-vehicle's motion trajectory from geometric evolution.
推进: Advance: 彻底扭转了世界模型从“生成像素”到“预测几何”的范式，在仿真环境中验证了基于几何演化的运动规划有效性。This completely reverses the paradigm of world models from 'generating pixels' to 'predicting geometry', validating the effectiveness of motion planning based on geometric evolution in simulation environments.
2026-09
Towards Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration of Household Devices
动机: Motivation: 为了解决视觉语言模型在具身探索中缺乏长程状态追踪与细粒度物理定位能力，试图建立可验证的逻辑驱动闭环。To address the lack of long-range state tracking and fine-grained physical localization capabilities in visual language models during embodied exploration, this work attempts to establish a verifiable logic-driven closed loop.
方法: Method: 构建VGEBench评估基准，设计逻辑驱动的状态机将物理反馈转化为可验证的闭环，利用3D模型库进行无手册设备探索。We construct the VGEBench evaluation benchmark, designing a logic-driven state machine to convert physical feedback into verifiable closed loops, utilizing 3D model libraries for manual-free device exploration.
推进: Advance: 揭示了当前VLM在抽象知识映射到具体物理操作上的短板，为通用具身探索提供了必要的量化评估与改进方向。This reveals the shortcomings of current VLMs in mapping abstract knowledge to specific physical operations, providing necessary quantitative evaluation and improvement directions for general embodied exploration.
2026-10
4Director: Controlling Video World Models with Rigid 3D Geometry
动机: Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在深度预测上存在严重歧义，常因依赖2D线索或近似几何表示（如高斯球）而导致旋转时出现形变，缺乏严格的物理一致性。Existing video world models suffer from severe ambiguity in depth prediction, often causing deformation during rotation due to reliance on 2D cues or approximate geometric representations (e.g., Gaussian spheres), lacking strict physical consistency.
方法: Method: 提出4Director框架，为每个物体构建完整的规范网格（canonical mesh），利用刚性几何约束替代像素级预测，并通过Motion Adapter将深度图转化为视频，将非刚性动态与外观细节交由生成器处理。Proposes the 4Director framework, constructing a complete canonical mesh for each object, utilizing rigid geometric constraints to replace pixel-level prediction, and using a Motion Adapter to convert depth maps into videos, leaving non-rigid dynamics and appearance details to the generator.
推进: Advance: 彻底消除了2D线索带来的深度歧义，实现了物体旋转时的严格几何一致性，并构建了包含2万条自动标注数据的RealCOD-Rigid数据集以验证刚性控制的有效性。Completely eliminates depth ambiguity caused by 2D cues, achieving strict geometric consistency during object rotation, and constructs the RealCOD-Rigid dataset containing 20,000 automatically annotated samples to validate the effectiveness of rigid control.

世界模型架构与可预测性优化World Model Architecture and Predictability Optimization

本角度聚焦于通过重构世界模型架构，以稀疏化表征和物理一致性约束提升预测效率与泛化能力。核心思路是从高维像素空间转向低维语义或几何空间（如骨架），在降低计算负载的同时强化对物理动态的建模。演进路径表现为从端到端视频预测向“骨架化”或“双视角”架构转型，旨在解决传统模型参数量大、对场景布局敏感及物理规律违背等痛点，实现参数效率与预测准确性的平衡。This angle focuses on reconstructing world model architectures to improve prediction efficiency and generalization through sparse representations and physical consistency constraints. The core idea is to shift from high-dimensional pixel space to low-dimensional semantic or geometric space (e.g., skeletons), strengthening the modeling of physical dynamics while reducing computational load. The evolutionary path manifests as a transition from end-to-end video prediction to "skeletonized" or "dual-view" architectures, aiming to solve pain points such as large parameter counts in traditional models, sensitivity to scene layout, and violation of physical laws, achieving a balance between parameter efficiency and prediction accuracy.

2026-10
SkeleWAM: Skeleton World-Action Modeling for Efficient Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 传统世界模型依赖高维像素预测，导致参数量巨大且对场景布局变化极度敏感，难以在资源受限的机器人上实现高效泛化。Traditional world models rely on high-dimensional pixel prediction, resulting in massive parameter counts and extreme sensitivity to scene layout changes, making efficient generalization on resource-constrained robots difficult.
方法: Method: 提出SkeleWAM架构，摒弃像素级视频预测，转而构建基于稀疏骨架的动作-状态世界模型，将复杂视觉信息压缩为几何骨架表征。Proposes the SkeleWAM architecture, abandoning pixel-level video prediction to instead construct an action-state world model based on sparse skeletons, compressing complex visual information into geometric skeleton representations.
推进: Advance: 成功将参数量从数十亿级压缩至5700万，显著提升了训练与推理效率，但在场景布局发生剧烈扰动时，其泛化性能出现明显下降。Successfully compressed parameter counts from billions to 57 million, significantly improving training and inference efficiency, but its generalization performance showed a noticeable decline when scene layouts underwent severe perturbations.
